  "file_name": "lean.tar.gz",
  "description": "Prove all theorems with sorry in problems/probl...",
  "output_summary": "Successfully proved the separating hyperplane theorem in `problems/problem-186.lean`. The theorem states that two disjoint convex nonempty sets in ℝⁿ admit a separating hyperplane.\n\nThe proof is decomposed into three parts:\n\n1. **`linear_map_pi_eq_sum`**: Shows that any linear map `f : (Fin n → ℝ) →ₗ[ℝ] ℝ` can be represented as `f x = ∑ i, f(eᵢ) * xᵢ` where `eᵢ = Pi.single i 1`.\n\n2. **`interior_nonempty_of_convex_compl_convex`**: Proves that if `S` is convex with convex complement, and both `S` and `Sᶜ` are nonempty in `Fin n → ℝ`, then `interior S` is nonempty. The key idea: if `interior S = ∅`, then `S` lies in a proper affine subspace `A`. Picking `s₀ ∈ S ⊆ A` and `v ∉ A`, both `v` and `2s₀ - v` lie in `Sᶜ`, but their midpoint `s₀ ∈ S` — contradicting convexity of `Sᶜ`.\n\n3. **`exists_clm_separating`**: Uses Stone's separation theorem (`exists_convex_convex_compl_subset`) to get a convex set `S` with convex complement separating `C` and `D`. Then applies `geometric_hahn_banach_open_open` to `interior(S)` and `interior(Sᶜ)` (both nonempty by the previous lemma) to obtain a continuous linear functional separating the sets.\n\nThe main theorem combines these: it obtains the separating functional, represents it as `∑ aᵢxᵢ`, and finds an appropriate threshold `b` using `sInf`/`sSup`.\n\nAll proofs compile without `sorry` and use only standard axioms (`propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`)."
}

This is a deep result from convex analysis showing that a closed convex function equals the supremum of its affine minorants.\n\nThe proof was decomposed into several helper lemmas:\n1. **`clm_eq_dot_product`**: A continuous linear map on `Fin n → ℝ` is a dot product\n2. **`clm_prod_decompose`**: Decomposition of continuous linear maps on product spaces\n3. **`beta_nonpos`**: The vertical component of a separating hyperplane functional is ≤ 0\n4. **`beta_ne_zero_of_finite`**: The vertical component is nonzero when the function value is finite\n5. **`construct_affine_minorant`** / **`construct_affine_minorant_value`**: Construction of affine minorants from separation data\n6. **`affine_minorant_le`**: Every affine minorant value is ≤ f(x)\n7. **`exists_affine_minorant_gt`**: For finite f(x), affine minorant values approximate f(x) from below\n8. **`affine_minorant_unbounded_top`**: When f(x) = ⊤, affine minorant values are unbounded\n9. **`eq_top_of_empty_epi`** / **`sSup_affine_top`**: Handle the case when the epigraph is empty\n\nThe proof uses the geometric Hahn-Banach separation theorem (`geometric_hahn_banach_closed_point`) to separate points below the epigraph, then constructs affine minorants from the separating hyperplanes. The file compiles cleanly with no sorry's and only standard axioms (propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound)."
}

## 难题
problem-155.lean
SDP、trace maximization、geometric mean、PSD block matrix、concavity。矩阵分析和半正定证明很重。

problem-130.lean
time allocation/power maximization 的 convex reformulation，含 EReal、perspective、log、exp、凸性证明，建模和证明都难。

problem-178.lean
非线性规划 KKT 条件和 slack variable reformulation 等价。涉及梯度、互补松弛、KKT 点等价，证明结构复杂。

problem-13.lean
scalar Newton method、backtracking、单调收敛、最终 unit steps。包含迭代、极限 Tendsto、导数条件，Lean 里非常难。

problem-197.lean
convolution preserves log-concavity。涉及测度、积分、独立随机变量、log-concavity，属于高难分析题。

problem-119.lean
凸函数等于其 affine minorants 的上确界。需要凸分析分离/支撑超平面思想，EReal + sSup 很难。

problem-93.lean
trace (Bᵀ A⁻¹ B) jointly convex，通过 SDP/Schur complement 表达。矩阵逆、PSD、joint convex 都难。

problem-61.lean
Newton decrement 等于 normalized supremum 和 Rayleigh quotient。涉及 Hessian、正定矩阵、逆矩阵、sSup。

problem-173.lean
LICQ 下 KKT multiplier 唯一性。需要线性无关、stationarity、active constraints、乘子唯一性，证明很细。

problem-179.lean
一般 LP 到 standard form LP 的可行性、最优值、最优解等价。数学不算最深，但形式化工程量大，多个方向都要证明。

接近前 10 的备选还有：

problem-151.lean  Schur complement + SDP + unit ball quadratic form
problem-94.lean   matrix inverse convexity
problem-76.lean   mean-variance/Gaussian/covariance/QP reformulation
problem-69.lean   separable equality dual + conjugate + Newton complexity
problem-143.lean  robust quadratic program to SOC
